def quantize_binary(h):
 n=len(h); k=n//2
 idx=np.argsort(-h)
 h2=np.zeros(n); h2[idx[:k]]=1.0
 return h2

Efficient binary local search with direct correlate int arrays:
```
def binary_local_search(h0, budget_frac, seed, max_attempts=None):
 n=len(h0); k=n//2
 h = h0.astype(np.int64).copy()
 b = 1 - h
 dx=2.0/n
 def eval(): return float(np.max(np.correlate(h,b,'full')))*dx
 c = eval()
 best_h=h.copy(); best_c=c
 ones=np.nonzero(h>0)[0]; zeros=np.nonzero(h==0)[0]
 t = 0.002?
 for it in range(N):
   # choose move
   if True:
     p = int(ones[rng.randint(len(ones))])
     q = int(zeros[rng.randint(len(zeros))])
   if p==q: continue
   h[p]-=1; h[q]+=1; b[p]-=1; b[q]+=1
   c2 = eval()
   accept = c2 < c or (rng.rand() < math.exp(-(c2-c)/T))
   if accept:
      c=c2
      if c2 < best_c - 1e-12:
         best_c=c2; best_h=h.copy()
      # update ones/zeros maybe
   else:
      h[p]+=1; h[q]-=1; b[p]+=1; b[q]-=1
   # adjust ones/zeros sets:
   if accepted: ones remove p add q; zeros remove q add p
   # Decrease T
```

`c5_exact(h)`:
```
def c5_exact(h):
    n=len(h); dx=2.0/n
    corr=np.correlate(h, 1.0-h, mode='full')
    return float(np.max(corr)*dx)
```
For speed, `b=1.0-h; np.correlate(h,b,'full')`.

`c5_from_hb(h,b)`: `return float(np.max(np.correlate(h,b,'full'))*(2.0/len(h)))`. For local search.

`project_feasible(h, target)`:
```
def project_feasible(h, target):
 h=np.asarray(h,float).copy()
 h=np.clip(h,0,1)
 # scale if far? target sum integer.
 for _ in range(60):
   diff=target-h.sum()
   if abs(diff)<1e-13: break
   if diff>0:
     mask=h<1.0
     cnt=mask.sum()
     if cnt==0: break
     h[mask]+=diff/cnt
   else:
     mask=h>0.0
     cnt=mask.sum()
     if cnt==0: break
     h[mask]+=diff/cnt
   h=np.clip(h,0,1)
 # exact fix
 diff=target-h.sum()
 if abs(diff)>1e-12:
   if diff>0:
     idx=np.where(h<1.0)[0]
   else:
     idx=np.where(h>0.0)[0]
   if len(idx)>0:
     # distribute diff among as many as needed? For small, one.
     for j in idx:
       if diff>0:
         add=min(diff,1.0-h[j])
       else: add=max(diff,h[j])
       h[j]+=add; diff-=add
       if abs(diff)<1e-13: break
 return h
```
But if residual huge due initial clipping, loop handles. For measure preserving binary quantize, sum exact target (k) no need project.

Param shape functions:
```
def shape_pw_h(n, p, w):
    dx=2.0/n
    x=(np.arange(n)+0.5)*dx
    s=np.abs(x-1.0)
    if w<=0: return np.full(n,0.5)
    if p<=0: p=1e-3
    wmax=min(1.0, (p+1.0)/(2.0*p)) # ensure A<=1
    w=min(w,wmax)
    if w<=1e-9:
       return np.full(n,0.5) # area1? constant 0.5
    t=s/w
    u=np.where(t<=1.0, 1.0 - np.power(t,p), 0.0)
    A=2.0*w*p/(p+1.0)
    v=(1.0-A)/(2.0-A)
    h=v + (1.0-v)*u
    return h
```
At w where A close 2? v? For A≤1, v≥0. If A very small, v close0.5. Good.

`smooth_top_grad(h, beta, top_k)`:
```
def smooth_top_grad(h, beta, top_k=16):
 n=len(h); dx=2.0/n
 b=1.0-h
 corr=np.correlate(h,b,mode='full')
 if top_k is None or top_k>=len(corr):
   idx=np.arange(len(corr))
 else:
   idx=np.argsort(corr)[-top_k:]
   # sort maybe not necessary
 c=corr[idx]; m=c.max(); shifted=c-m
 exps=np.exp(beta*shifted)
 w=exps/exps.sum()
 val=(m + np.log(exps.sum())/beta)*dx
 grad=np.zeros(n)
 base=n-1
 for idx_k, wt in zip(idx, w):
   if wt<1e-12: continue
   k=int(idx_k-base)
   if k>=0:
     i_lo=k; i_hi=n-1
   else:
     i_lo=0; i_hi=n-1+k
   if i_lo>i_hi: continue
   m_vals=np.arange(i_lo, i_hi+1)
   j_vals=m_vals-k
   grad[m_vals] += wt*(1.0-h[j_vals])
   grad[j_vals] += -wt*h[m_vals]
 grad *= dx
 return val, grad
```
This loops top_k arrays total O(top_n). Could be okay. top_k maybe 8-32.

b=1-h; c=exact
best...
mom=zeros
decay=0.85
alpha=0.05
for it:
  val,g=smooth_top_grad(h,beta,top_k)
  gnorm=np.linalg.norm(g)
  if gnorm<1e-12: break
  g_norm=g/gnorm
  mom = decay*mom - alpha*g_norm
  # maybe scale mom by a step based on variance? h range [0,1], alpha ~0.05
  candidate = h + mom
  candidate = project_feasible(candidate, target)
  c2=exact
  if c2 < c:
     h=candidate; c=c2; update best
  else:
     # try half
     cand2 = h + 0.5*mom; project; c2=exact
     if c2 < c: h=cand2; ...
     else: # keep h, but maybe still reset mom? 
  alpha*=? decay? 
```

def continuous_random_polish(h0, seed, time_limit):
 n=len(h0); if n>600: return h0
 h=project_feasible(h0,target)
 b=1-h; c=eval; best...
 T0 = 1e-4? Objective continuous improvements ~1e-5. For random moves, maybe accept improvements only. We'll use greedy plus simulated.
 T=1e-3
 for it:
   i=rng.randint(n); j=rng.randint(n); if i==j continue
   # choose direction: move mass from high to low? two possibilities
   # Use random delta from list, but ensure bounds
   for delta in [0.002,0.005,0.01,0.02,0.05,0.001]:
      if h[i]>h[j]: # move some from i to j? maybe increase lower, decrease upper

      else: h_i +=delta, h_j -=delta
      apply if bounds.
      c2=eval
      if c2 < c - 1e-15 or accept:
          accept; c=c2; break
      revert
   if no imv many, reduce T? 
```

```
def optimize_shape_p1(n_eval, time_limit):
  global _GLOBAL_SHAPE_N
  _GLOBAL_SHAPE_N=n_eval
  bestw=0.95; bestc=1e9
  ws = np.arange(0.70, 1.000001, 0.01)
  for w in ws:
    c=shape_pw_obj([1.0,float(w)])
    if c<bestc: bestc=c; bestw=float(w)
  # local refine
  for _ in range(3):
    step=0.005
    improved=False
    for dw in np.linspace(-step,step,17):
      w=test=bestw+dw
      if 0<w<=1:
        c=shape_pw_obj([1.0,float(w)])
        if c<bestc: ... improved True
    step*=0.4
  return bestw,bestc
```

Implement segment search:
```
def segment_search(h1,h2, n, seed, time_limit):
 if len(h1)!=len(h2): h2=resample(h2,len(h1))
 n=len(h1); target=n/2
 b1=1-h1; b2=1-h2
 for alpha in [0,0.1,...,1]:
   h=project_feasible((1-alpha)h1+alpha h2,target)
   c=c5_exact(h); consider
```

Local polish implementation details:

`gradient_polish(h0, beta_list, top_k, time_limit)`:
Need check time using global `_DEADLINE`. We set `_DEADLINE` in run. Function returns h,best_c maybe.
```
def gradient_polish(h0, beta_list=(50,100,200,400,800,1500), top_k=24, time_limit=0.0):
 n=len(h0); target=n/2.0; h=project_feasible(h0,target)
 b=1-h; c=c5_from_hb(h,b); best=h.copy(); bestc=c
 mom=np.zeros(n); alpha=0.05; decay=0.85
 for beta in beta_list:
   if time.time() > _DEADLINE - 1: break
   for it in range(200):
     if time.time() > _DEADLINE - 1: break
     val,g=smooth_top_grad(h,beta,top_k)
     gn=np.linalg.norm(g)
     if gn < 1e-12: break
     g/=gn
     mom=decay*mom - alpha*g
     # line search factors [1,0.5,0.25]
     accepted=False
     for fac in [1.0,0.5,0.25,0.125]:
       trial=h+fac*mom
       trial=project_feasible(trial,target)
       btrial=1-trial
       ct=c5_from_hb(trial,btrial)
       if ct < c - 1e-15:
          h=trial; b=btrial; c=ct; accepted=True
          if ct < bestc: bestc=ct; best=trial.copy()
          break
     if not accepted:
       # reduce alpha occasionally
       alpha *= 0.995
       # maybe accept no, but keep momentum
   alpha = max(0.005, alpha*0.5?)
 return best,bestc
```
This can be slow: each iteration top_k arrays and line search maybe 2-3 exact correlates. n=512, top_k=24 -> for each iter: smooth grad O(24*512) ~12k, exact 3*100k =300k. 200* maybe 10M ops, seconds. Many betas 6*200=1200 => ~4s? Actually correlate 512 direct O260k, 3*1200=774M ops maybe seconds tens. Budget okay. But if n=512 and 600s, good.

But if h at n=1024 too slow; we'll restrict gradient polish to n≤400 by resampling if larger.

`continuous_random_polish`:
```
def random_polish(h0, seed, time_limit):
 n=len(h0); target=n/2.0
 h=project_feasible(h0,target); b=1-h; c=eval; best...
 deltas = [0.001,0.002,0.003,0.005,0.008,0.012,0.02,0.04]
 T=1e-5? For continuous objective, improvement small; accept improvements only mostly. Use T=1e-4 maybe.
 no_improve=0
 while time:
   i=rng.randint(n); j=rng.randint(n); if i==j continue
   # choose direction biased to reduce variance
   if h[i] > h[j]:
      di = -deltas[rng]; dj=-di
   elif h[j] > h[i]:
      di = deltas[rng]; dj=-di
   else: random
   if h[i]+di <0 or >1 or h[j]+dj... continue
   apply; c2=eval; accept if c2<c or rng<exp(- (c2-c)/T)
   if accept: update; if c2<bestc no_improve=0 else maybe no_improve?; if c2<bestc bestc...
   else revert
   if no_improve>20000: T*=0.5 etc; reset no_improve
```
But exact correlate per iteration for n=512 ~100k ops. 1M iterations =100M C? maybe 50s. Actually 1e6*0.0001s=100s. Good. We can set time. Python loop overhead maybe 5us + correlate 50us =55us, 200k in 10s. Fine.

`binary_local_search`:
Will be faster with int correlate. We'll implement and run for maybe 200s. Use `h` int64, `b` int64. `np.correlate` with int64 output int64, max int64; dx float. `c = float(np.max(corr))*dx`.
Moves: random p/q. We'll maintain `no_improve` and T. Since moves integer, T in objective units.
```
def binary_local_search(h0, seed, time_limit):
 n=len(h0)
 if n%2: n-=1? h0 length even due target. 
 h=np.zeros(n,int); from top. But input may be binary? We'll quantize before.
 b=1-h
 c=eval; best...
 T=0.02
 ones_count=n//2
 while time:
   p=rng.randint(n); while h[p]==0: p=...
   q=rng.randint(n); while h[q]==1: q=...
   if p==q: continue
   h[p]-=1;h[q]+=1;b[p]-=1;b[q]+=1
   c2=eval
   if c2 < c:
      accept
      if c2<bestc: bestc=c2; best=h.copy(); no_improve=0
   else:
      if rng.random() < math.exp(-(c2-c)/T): accept; else revert
      if not accept: no_improve += 1
   if no_improve>5000: T=max(0.001,T*0.5); no_improve=0
   # every 10000: T decay
   T=max(1e-5, T*0.9999)
```
But maintaining no_improve if accept uphill but not best; should reset if c improved relative? okay.
However, binary search from threshold of continuous shape is bad (central interval). Starting from parent may be better. We'll quantize parent if parent exists, also maybe quantize a *randomized* version of best? Instead of top K, to avoid bad central interval, we can randomize tie/top: choose n/2 indices with probability proportional to h? Top exactly. Parent likely not central if good. If shape parent h central high, top interval bad. We shouldn't binary search shape. But binary search parent maybe.

Quantize parent: if parent has values, top n/2. Could be good. If not, use no binary.

def optimize_shape_pw(n_eval, min_w_frac=0.75, time_budget=None):
    global _GLOBAL_SHAPE_N
    _GLOBAL_SHAPE_N = n_eval
    best_c = 1e9; best_p=1.0; best_w=1.0
    p_list = [0.7,0.8,1.0,1.2,1.5,2.0]
    w_list base = [0.8,0.85,0.9,0.95,1.0]
    for p in p_list:
       pmax = min(1.0, (p+1)/(2*p))
       if pmax <=0: continue
       for wf in w_list:
          w = pmax*wf
          # ensure a few near 1
          c = shape_pw_obj([p,w])
          update
    # local refine around best: p ±0.1 maybe, w ±0.03 steps
    for rad_p, rad_w, step in [(0.15,0.04,0.02), (0.05,0.015,0.005), (0.02,0.005,0.0015)]:
       if time_left()<2: break
       improved=False
       ns_p = int(rad_p/step)+1 maybe; ns_w similarly
       for dp in np.arange(-rad_p, rad_p+step, step):
          p=best_p+dp
          if p<0.3 or p>3: continue
          pmax = min(1.0, (p+1)/(2*p))
          # keep w within pmax; also compare scaled w relative to pmax? If best_w > pmax, clamp? Need map. Simpler evaluate w=best_w+jstep if <=pmax.
          for dw in np.arange(-rad_w, rad_w+step, step):
             w=best_w+dw
             if w<=0 or w>pmax: continue
             c=...
       if not improved: break
    # final evaluate at n_eval? Return best_p,best_w,best_c
    return best_p,best_w,best_c
```

```
def make_feasible_candidate(h):
    if h is None: return None,1e9,None
    h=np.asarray(h,float)
    if h.size==0: return None,1e9,None
    n=len(h)
    target=n/2.0
    # ensure n even? If n odd, target integer? Task sum(n_points/2) may be half integer; h values can be floats. Use n as len. We probably use even n. For safety.
    h=project_feasible(h,target)
    c=c5_exact(h)
    return h,c,n
```

Detailed function definitions:

```
import numpy as np
import time
import math

_DEADLINE = 0.0
_GLOBAL_SHAPE_N = 512

def time_left():
    return max(0.0, _DEADLINE - time.time())
```

`project_feasible`:
```
def project_feasible(h, target):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float).copy()
    h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)
    for _ in range(100):
        s = h.sum()
        diff = target - s
        if abs(diff) < 1e-14:
            break
        if diff > 0:
            mask = h < 1.0
        else:
            mask = h > 0.0
        cnt = int(np.count_nonzero(mask))
        if cnt == 0:
            break
        step = diff / cnt
        h[mask] += step
        h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)
    diff = target - h.sum()
    if abs(diff) > 1e-12:
        if diff > 0:
            idx = np.where(h < 1.0)[0]
        else:
            idx = np.where(h > 0.0)[0]
        for j in idx:
            if diff > 0:
                add = min(diff, 1.0 - h[j])
            else:
                add = max(diff, -h[j])  # negative
            h[j] += add
            diff -= add
            if abs(diff) < 1e-13:
                break
    return h
```
Potential: if diff>0 and add smaller, diff updated. If diff negative, max(diff,-h[j]) gives negative amount not removing more than h[j]. good.

`c5_exact(h)`:
```
def c5_exact(h):
    n = len(h)
    if n == 0: return 1e9
    b = 1.0 - h
    corr = np.correlate(h, b, mode='full')
    return float(np.max(corr) * (2.0 / n))
```
`c5_from_hb(h,b)` similar.

`make_feasible_candidate(h)`:
```
def make_feasible_candidate(h):
    if h is None:
       return None, 1e9, 0
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float)
    if h.size == 0:
       return None,1e9,0
    n=len(h)
    h=project_feasible(h, n/2.0)
    c=c5_exact(h)
    return h,c,n
```
If n=1? target .5; okay. But `np.correlate` length2. Fine.

`transformed_variants(h)`:
```
def transformed_variants(h):
    h=np.asarray(h,float)
    if len(h)==0: return []
    return [h.copy(), 1.0-h.copy(), h[::-1].copy(), (1.0-h[::-1]).copy()]
```
Complement of h must area? If h area n/2, complement area n-n/2=n/2, okay.

`resample_to_n(h, n_new)`:
```
def resample_to_n(h, n_new):
    if n_new <=0: return h
    n0=len(h)
    if n0==n_new:
       return project_feasible(h, n0/2.0)
    dx0=2.0/n0
    xp=(np.arange(n0)+0.5)*dx0
    xn=(np.arange(n_new)+0.5)*2.0/n_new
    hnew=np.interp(xn,xp,h)
    return project_feasible(hnew,n_new/2.0)
```

`shape_pw_h(n,p,w)` as above. Need ensure p finite. If w<0, treat 0. If p<0.01 set 0.01. wmax = min(1.0, (p+1.0)/(2.0*p)); if p small max >1 cap1. If w>wmax, w=wmax. If A computed >1 due rounding, cap. `A = 2*w*p/(p+1)`. If A>=2? not with cap. v=(1-A)/(2-A); if v<0 set0 and maybe u/u? If v<0 due A>1, exp? We cap.
```
if w <= 1e-12: return np.full(n,0.5)
```
But if p large and wmax maybe .5, okay.

```
t=s/w
u=np.where(t<=1.0, 1.0 - np.power(np.clip(t,0,1),p), 0.0)
```
At t=0, power 0, u=1. Good. For p non-integer and t small positive, ok.

`shape_pw_obj(params)`:
```
def shape_pw_obj(params):
    p=float(params[0]); w=float(params[1])
    if not (np.isfinite(p) and np.isfinite(w)) or p <= 0.0 or w <= 0.0:
        return 1e6
    n=int(_GLOBAL_SHAPE_N)
    h=shape_pw_h(n,p,w)
    h=project_feasible(h, n/2.0)
    return c5_exact(h)
```

def optimize_shape_p1(n_eval, full=True):
    global _GLOBAL_SHAPE_N
    _GLOBAL_SHAPE_N = n_eval
    bestw=0.95; bestc=1e9
    if full:
      ws=np.linspace(0.80,1.0,41)
      for w in ws: ...
    # local refinement
    step=0.005
    for _ in range(5):
      improved=False
      for w in np.linspace(bestw-step, bestw+step, 21):
        if 0.01 < w <=1.0:
          c=shape_pw_obj([1.0,float(w)])
          if c < bestc - 1e-15: bestc=c; bestw=float(w); improved=True
      if not improved: break
      step *= 0.5
    return float(bestw), float(bestc)
```

`smooth_top_grad` implement. Need maybe if top_k large, sorting corr O(n log n). For n 400 fine. Use `idx = np.argpartition(corr, -top_k)[-top_k:]` to be faster? Then no need sorted. Use if top_k < len. `idx = np.argpartition(corr, len(corr)-top_k)[-top_k:]`. Good.
```
def smooth_top_grad(h, beta, top_k=24):
 n=len(h); dx=2.0/n; b=1.0-h
 corr=np.correlate(h,b,mode='full')
 m=len(corr)
 if top_k is None or top_k>=m:
   idx=np.arange(m); vals=corr
 else:
   idx=np.argpartition(corr, m-top_k)[-top_k:]
   vals=corr[idx]
 maxv=vals.max()
 shifted=vals-maxv
 exps=np.exp(beta*shifted)
 s=exps.sum()
 w=exps/s
 val=(maxv + math.log(s)/beta)*dx
 grad=np.zeros(n)
 base=n-1
 for ii in range(len(idx)):
   wt=float(w[ii])
   if wt < 1e-13: continue
   k=int(idx[ii]) - base
   if k >=0:
     i_lo=k; i_hi=n-1
   else:
     i_lo=0; i_hi=n-1+k
   if i_lo > i_hi: continue
   m_vals=np.arange(i_lo, i_hi+1)
   j_vals=m_vals - k
   grad[m_vals] += wt * (1.0 - h[j_vals])
   grad[j_vals] += -wt * h[m_vals]
 grad *= dx
 return val, grad
```
For k negative, i_hi = n-1+k (since k=-l => n-1-l), length n-l. Good. j_vals = m_vals - k = i - (-l)=i+l within range.

`gradient_polish`:
```
def gradient_polish(h0, beta_list=(30,60,120,240,480), top_k=20):
 n=len(h0); if n<4 return h0
 target=n/2.0
 h=project_feasible(h0,target)
 c=c5_exact(h)
 best=h.copy(); bestc=c
 mom=np.zeros(n)
 for beta in beta_list:
   if time_left()<3: break
   alpha=0.03
   for it in range(120):
     if time_left()<3: break
     val,g=smooth_top_grad(h,beta,top_k)
     gn=float(np.linalg.norm(g))
     if gn < 1e-14: break
     g = g/gn
     mom = 0.85*mom - alpha*g
     accepted=False
     for fac in (1.0,0.5,0.25,0.125):
       trial=h + fac*mom
       trial=project_feasible(trial,target)
       ct=c5_exact(trial)
       if ct < c - 1e-15:
          h=trial; c=ct; accepted=True
          if ct < bestc -1e-15:
             bestc=ct; best=h.copy()
          break
     if not accepted:
       alpha *= 0.97
       if alpha < 0.005: alpha=0.005
   alpha = max(0.5*alpha, 0.005)
 return best,bestc
```
Line search uses exact c each trial; no need b. For n=400 and 5*120=600*4=2400 eval. Maybe okay. If too slow, time guard. Could reduce to 60 iter. But shape already good; we can skip if best_c<0.36? Could still use. To save, call only if time >250 and best_n≤400. If n lower, okay.

`random_polish`:
```
def random_polish(h0, seed=123):
 n=len(h0); if n<8 return h0
 target=n/2
 h=project_feasible(h0,target)
 c=c5_exact(h); best=h.copy(); bestc=c
 rng=np.random.RandomState(seed)
 deltas=np.array([0.001,0.002,0.003,0.005,0.008,0.012,0.02])
 T=1e-4
 no=0
 while time_left()>3:
   i=rng.randint(n); j=rng.randint(n)
   if i==j: continue
   d=float(deltas[rng.randint(len(deltas))])
   # direction: with prob 0.7 move from high to low (i as high? choose)
   if rng.rand() < 0.6 and h[i] > h[j]:
      di=-d; dj=d
   elif rng.rand() < 0.6 and h[j] > h[i]:
      di=d; dj=-d
   elif h[i] < 1.0 and h[j] > 0.0 and rng.rand()<0.5:
      di=d; dj=-d
   else:
      di=-d; dj=d
   if h[i]+di < -1e-12 or h[i]+di > 1.0+1e-12:
      continue
   if h[j]+dj < -1e-12 or h[j]+dj > 1.0+1e-12:
      continue
   h[i]+=di; h[j]+=dj
   c2=c5_exact(h)
   accept = c2 < c - 1e-15
   if not accept and c2 - c < T:
       if math.exp(-(c2-c)/T) > rng.rand(): accept=True
   if accept:
      c=c2
      if c < bestc -1e-15:
         bestc=c; best=h.copy(); no=0
      else:
         no += 1
   else:
      h[i]-=di; h[j]-=dj
      no +=1
   if no > 5000:
      T=max(1e-6,T*0.5); no=0
 return best,bestc
```
Each eval project not needed because moves preserve sum and bounds; sum floating drift maybe. We can every 10000 project:
```
if no%10000==0: h=project_feasible(h,target); c=c5_exact(h); ...
```
But if project changes c, need update. Simpler not project except at end; sum preserved in floating to ~1e-15 per iteration; after many maybe drift. We can every 10000 iterations using counter.
Implement with `it` counter. If project, use `c=c5_exact(h)` and if c<bestc update.

`binary_local_search` as above. Need `h0` possibly not binary; quantize before.
```
def quantize_binary(h):
 n=len(h); k=n//2
 if n%2: # target half? For binary cannot sum .5. Use floor/ceil? We'll only use even.
   k=n//2
 idx=np.argsort(-h)[:k]
 h2=np.zeros(n); h2[idx]=1.0
 return h2
```
If n odd, binary sum integer not target .5; candidate invalid. We'll only call for even. In `make_feasible_candidate`, project would alter 0/1 to satisfy .5, making fractional. Better for binary local search, only even n. If parent n odd, skip or even resample.

Binary objective: `np.correlate` with int64. Usec5_exact float works. Use `b = 1 - h` int? If h int64, `1.0-h` float? For int, b = 1 - h (int64). Then `np.correlate(h,b)` int. For c:
```
def c5_int(h,b):
 return float(np.max(np.correlate(h,b,mode='full'))*(2.0/len(h)))
```
Top-level.

`binary_local_search`:
```
def binary_local_search(h0, seed=9):
 n=len(h0)
 if n%2 !=0: return h0, 1e9
 h=np.zeros(n,dtype=np.int64)
 k=n//2
 idx=np.argsort(-h0)[:k]
 h[idx]=1
 b=1-h
 c=c5_int(h,b)
 best=h.copy(); bestc=c
 rng=np.random.RandomState(seed)
 T=0.02
 no=0
 it=0
 while time_left()>3:
   it+=1
   p=rng.randint(n)
   while h[p]==0: p=rng.randint(n)
   q=rng.randint(n)
   while h[q]==1: q=rng.randint(n)
   if p==q: continue
   h[p]-=1; h[q]+=1; b[p]-=1; b[q]+=1
   c2=c5_int(h,b)
   accept=c2 < c - 1e-15
   if not accept and c2 - c <= T and math.exp(-(c2-c)/T) > rng.rand(): accept=True
   if accept:
      c=c2
      if c < bestc -1e-15:
         bestc=c; best=h.copy(); no=0
      else: no+=1
   else:
      h[p]+=1; h[q]-=1; b[p]+=1; b[q]+=1
      no+=1
   if it % 5000 ==0:
      T=max(0.001, T*0.9)
   if no > 20000:
      T=max(0.01, T*2.0)? Maybe increase? Simulated anneal usually decrease. If stuck, maybe restart? We can keep.
```
If c2-c > T, `math.exp` underflow; check `c2-c <= T` else skip. T in objective units. For n=160 dx=.0125, T=.02 allows 1-increase prob ~0.85? exp(-.0125/.02)=.535. Good. Decrease to .001 quickly. If c2-c large (.1), not accept.
This search might be slow due while if no ones/zeros? always half. Good.

`segment_search`:
```
def segment_search(h1,h2, algas=None):
 n1=len(h1); n2=len(h2)
 if n1 != n2:
    h2=resample_to_n(h2,n1)
 else:
    h2=np.asarray(h2)
 n=n1; target=n/2
 h1=project_feasible(h1,target); h2=project_feasible(h2,target)
 if algas is None: algas=np.linspace(0,1,11)
 bestc=1e9; best=None
 for a in algas:
   h=project_feasible((1-a)*h1 + a*h2,target)
   c=c5_exact(h)
   if c<bestc: bestc=c; best=h.copy()
 return best,bestc
```

`random_polish` counter. Let's implement carefully:
```
def random_polish(h0, seed=123):
 n=len(h0)
 if n<8: return h0, 1e9
 target=n/2.0
 h=project_feasible(h0,target)
 c=c5_exact(h)
 best=h.copy(); bestc=c
 rng=np.random.RandomState(seed)
 deltas=np.array([0.001,0.002,0.003,0.005,0.008,0.012,0.02])
 T=5e-4
 no=0; it=0
 while time_left() > 3:
   it +=1
   i=int(rng.randint(n)); j=int(rng.randint(n))
   if i==j: continue
   d=float(deltas[int(rng.randint(len(deltas)))])
   if rng.rand() < 0.65:
      move_hi_to_lo = True
   else: move_hi_to_lo=False
   if move_hi_to_lo:
      if h[i] > h[j]: di=-d; dj=d
      elif h[j] > h[i]: di=d; dj=-d
      else: di=d; dj=-d
   else:
      if rng.rand()<0.5: di=d; dj=-d
      else: di=-d; dj=d
   if h[i]+di < -1e-12 or h[i]+di > 1.0+1e-12: continue
   if h[j]+dj < -1e-12 or h[j]+dj > 1.0+1e-12: continue
   h[i]+=di; h[j]+=dj
   c2=c5_exact(h)
   accept = (c2 < c - 1e-15)
   if not accept and c2 - c < T:
      if math.exp(-(c2-c)/T) > rng.rand(): accept=True
   if accept:
      c=c2
      if c < bestc - 1e-15: bestc=c; best=h.copy(); no=0
      else: no+=1
   else:
      h[i]-=di; h[j]-=dj
      no+=1
   if it % 20000 == 0:
      h=project_feasible(h,target)
      c=c5_exact(h)
      if c < bestc - 1e-15: bestc=c; best=h.copy()
   if no > 8000:
      T = max(1e-5, T*0.5); no=0
   elif no < 2000:
      T = min(0.01, T*1.02)
 return best,bestc
```
This T adaptation maybe. Good.

```python
import numpy as np
import time
import math

_DEADLINE = 0.0
_GLOBAL_SHAPE_N = 512


def time_left():
    return max(0.0, _DEADLINE - time.time())


def project_feasible(h, target):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float).copy()
    h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)
    for _ in range(80):
        s = h.sum()
        diff = target - s
        if abs(diff) < 1e-14:
            break
        if diff > 0:
            mask = h < 1.0
        else:
            mask = h > 0.0
        cnt = int(np.count_nonzero(mask))
        if cnt == 0:
            break
        h[mask] += diff / cnt
        h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)

    diff = target - h.sum()
    if abs(diff) > 1e-12:
        if diff > 0:
            idx = np.where(h < 1.0)[0]
        else:
            idx = np.where(h > 0.0)[0]
        for j in idx:
            if diff > 0:
                add = min(diff, 1.0 - h[j])
            else:
                add = max(diff, -h[j])
            h[j] += add
            diff -= add
            if abs(diff) < 1e-13:
                break
    return h


def c5_exact(h):
    n = len(h)
    if n == 0:
        return 1e9
    b = 1.0 - h
    corr = np.correlate(h, b, mode='full')
    return float(np.max(corr) * (2.0 / n))


def c5_int(h, b):
    n = len(h)
    if n == 0:
        return 1e9
    corr = np.correlate(h, b, mode='full')
    return float(np.max(corr) * (2.0 / n))


def make_feasible_candidate(h):
    if h is None:
        return None, 1e9, 0
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float)
    if h.size == 0:
        return None, 1e9, 0
    n = len(h)
    h = project_feasible(h, n / 2.0)
    c = c5_exact(h)
    return h, c, n


def resample_to_n(h, n_new):
    if n_new <= 0:
        return h
    n0 = len(h)
    if n0 == n_new:
        return project_feasible(np.asarray(h, dtype=float), n0 / 2.0)
    dx0 = 2.0 / n0
    xp = (np.arange(n0) + 0.5) * dx0
    xn = (np.arange(n_new) + 0.5) * (2.0 / n_new)
    hnew = np.interp(xn, xp, np.asarray(h, dtype=float))
    return project_feasible(hnew, n_new / 2.0)


def transformed_variants(h):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float)
    if h.size == 0:
        return []
    return [
        h.copy(),
        (1.0 - h).copy(),
        h[::-1].copy(),
        (1.0 - h[::-1]).copy()
    ]


def shape_pw_h(n, p, w):
    n = int(n)
    p = float(p)
    w = float(w)
    if p <= 0.0:
        p = 1e-3
    if w <= 0.0:
        return np.full(n, 0.5, dtype=float)

    if not np.isfinite(p) or not np.isfinite(w):
        return np.full(n, 0.5, dtype=float)

    pmax = (p + 1.0) / (2.0 * p)
    if not np.isfinite(pmax) or pmax <= 0.0:
        pmax = 1.0
    pmin = min(1.0, pmax)
    if w > pmin:
        w = pmin

    dx = 2.0 / n
    x = (np.arange(n) + 0.5) * dx
    s = np.abs(x - 1.0)

    if w <= 1e-12:
        return np.full(n, 0.5, dtype=float)

    t = s / w
    u = np.where(t <= 1.0, 1.0 - np.power(np.clip(t, 0.0, 1.0), p), 0.0)

    A = 2.0 * w * p / (p + 1.0)
    if A > 1.0:
        A = 1.0
    v = (1.0 - A) / (2.0 - A)
    if v < 0.0:
        v = 0.0
    elif v > 0.5:
        v = 0.5

    h = v + (1.0 - v) * u
    return h


def shape_pw_obj(params):
    p = float(params[0])
    w = float(params[1])
    if not np.isfinite(p) or not np.isfinite(w) or p <= 0.0 or w <= 0.0:
        return 1e6
    n = int(_GLOBAL_SHAPE_N)
    h = shape_pw_h(n, p, w)
    h = project_feasible(h, n / 2.0)
    return c5_exact(h)


def optimize_shape_p1(n_eval):
    global _GLOBAL_SHAPE_N
    _GLOBAL_SHAPE_N = int(n_eval)

    bestw = 0.95
    bestc = 1e9

    ws = np.linspace(0.78, 1.0, 49)
    for w in ws:
        if time_left() < 2.0:
            break
        w = float(w)
        if w <= 0.0 or w > 1.0:
            continue
        c = shape_pw_obj([1.0, w])
        if c < bestc:
            bestc = c
            bestw = w

    step = 0.005
    for _ in range(6):
        if time_left() < 1.0:
            break
        improved = False
        for w in np.linspace(max(0.01, bestw - step), min(1.0, bestw + step), 21):
            w = float(w)
            if w <= 0.0:
                continue
            c = shape_pw_obj([1.0, w])
            if c < bestc - 1e-15:
                bestc = c
                bestw = w
                improved = True
        if not improved:
            break
        step *= 0.5

    return float(bestw), float(bestc)


def optimize_shape_pw(n_eval):
    global _GLOBAL_SHAPE_N
    _GLOBAL_SHAPE_N = int(n_eval)

    bestp = 1.0
    bestw = 1.0
    bestc = 1e9

    p_list = [0.7, 0.8, 0.9, 1.0, 1.1, 1.2, 1.4, 1.7, 2.0]
    frac_list = [0.75, 0.80, 0.85, 0.90, 0.95, 1.0]

    for p in p_list:
        if time_left() < 2.0:
            break
        p = float(p)
        pmax = (p + 1.0) / (2.0 * p)
        pmax = min(1.0, pmax)
        for fr in frac_list:
            w = pmax * float(fr)
            if w <= 0.0:
                continue
            c = shape_pw_obj([p, w])
            if c < bestc:
                bestc = c
                bestp = p
                bestw = w

    pmax_best = min(1.0, (bestp + 1.0) / (2.0 * bestp))

    stepw = 0.01
    for _ in range(5):
        if time_left() < 1.0:
            break
        improved = False
        wlo = max(1e-3, bestw - stepw)
        whi = min(pmax_best, bestw + stepw)
        for w in np.linspace(wlo, whi, 21):
            c = shape_pw_obj([bestp, float(w)])
            if c < bestc - 1e-15:
                bestc = c
                bestw = float(w)
                improved = True
        if not improved:
            break
        stepw *= 0.5

    stepp = 0.03
    bestw_keep = bestw
    pmax_best = min(1.0, (bestp + 1.0) / (2.0 * bestp))
    for _ in range(4):
        if time_left() < 1.0:
            break
        improved = False
        plo = max(0.4, bestp - stepp)
        phi = min(3.0, bestp + stepp)
        for p in np.linspace(plo, phi, 17):
            p = float(p)
            pmax = min(1.0, (p + 1.0) / (2.0 * p))
            if pmax <= 0.0:
                continue
            w_prop = bestw_keep * (pmax / pmax_best) if pmax_best > 0 else pmax
            for w in (w_prop, bestw_keep):
                w = float(w)
                if w <= 1e-4 or w > pmax:
                    continue
                c = shape_pw_obj([p, w])
                if c < bestc - 1e-15:
                    bestc = c
                    bestp = p
                    bestw = w
                    bestw_keep = w
                    improved = True
        if not improved:
            break
        stepp *= 0.5

    pmax_best = min(1.0, (bestp + 1.0) / (2.0 * bestp))
    stepw = 0.005
    for _ in range(4):
        if time_left() < 1.0:
            break
        improved = False
        wlo = max(1e-3, bestw - stepw)
        whi = min(pmax_best, bestw + stepw)
        for w in np.linspace(wlo, whi, 13):
            c = shape_pw_obj([bestp, float(w)])
            if c < bestc - 1e-15:
                bestc = c
                bestw = float(w)
                improved = True
        if not improved:
            break
        stepw *= 0.5

    return float(bestp), float(bestw), float(bestc)


def smooth_top_grad(h, beta, top_k=20):
    n = len(h)
    dx = 2.0 / n
    b = 1.0 - h
    corr = np.correlate(h, b, mode='full')
    m = len(corr)
    if top_k is None or top_k >= m:
        idx = np.arange(m)
    else:
        idx = np.argpartition(corr, m - int(top_k))[-int(top_k):]

    vals = corr[idx]
    mv = float(vals.max())
    shifted = vals - mv
    exps = np.exp(beta * shifted)
    s = float(exps.sum())
    wts = exps / s
    val = (mv + math.log(s) / beta) * dx

    grad = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    base = n - 1
    for ii in range(len(idx)):
        wt = float(wts[ii])
        if wt < 1e-13:
            continue
        k = int(idx[ii]) - base
        if k >= 0:
            i_lo = k
            i_hi = n - 1
        else:
            i_lo = 0
            i_hi = n - 1 + k
        if i_lo > i_hi:
            continue
        mvals = np.arange(i_lo, i_hi + 1)
        jvals = mvals - k
        grad[mvals] += wt * (1.0 - h[jvals])
        grad[jvals] += -wt * h[mvals]
    grad *= dx
    return val, grad


def gradient_polish(h0):
    n = len(h0)
    if n < 4:
        return h0, 1e9
    target = n / 2.0
    h = project_feasible(np.asarray(h0, dtype=float), target)
    c = c5_exact(h)
    best = h.copy()
    bestc = c
    mom = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)

    beta_list = [30.0, 60.0, 120.0, 240.0, 400.0]
    top_k = 20
    for beta in beta_list:
        if time_left() < 4.0:
            break
        alpha = 0.03
        for _ in range(90):
            if time_left() < 4.0:
                break
            _, g = smooth_top_grad(h, beta, top_k)
            gn = float(np.linalg.norm(g))
            if gn < 1e-14:
                break
            g = g / gn
            mom = 0.85 * mom - alpha * g
            accepted = False
            for fac in (1.0, 0.5, 0.25, 0.125):
                trial = h + fac * mom
                trial = project_feasible(trial, target)
                ct = c5_exact(trial)
                if ct < c - 1e-15:
                    h = trial
                    c = ct
                    accepted = True
                    if ct < bestc - 1e-15:
                        bestc = ct
                        best = h.copy()
                    break
            if not accepted:
                alpha *= 0.98
                if alpha < 0.005:
                    alpha = 0.005
        alpha = max(0.005, alpha * 0.5)

    return best, bestc


def random_polish(h0, seed=1234):
    n = len(h0)
    if n < 8:
        return h0, 1e9
    target = n / 2.0
    h = project_feasible(np.asarray(h0, dtype=float), target)
    c = c5_exact(h)
    best = h.copy()
    bestc = c
    rng = np.random.RandomState(int(seed))
    deltas = np.array([0.001, 0.002, 0.003, 0.005, 0.008, 0.012, 0.02], dtype=float)
    T = 5e-4
    no = 0
    it = 0

    while time_left() > 4.0:
        it += 1
        i = int(rng.randint(n))
        j = int(rng.randint(n))
        if i == j:
            continue
        d = float(deltas[int(rng.randint(len(deltas)))])

        move_hi_lo = rng.rand() < 0.65
        if move_hi_lo:
            if h[i] > h[j]:
                di = -d
                dj = d
            elif h[j] > h[i]:
                di = d
                dj = -d
            else:
                di = d
                dj = -d
        else:
            if rng.rand() < 0.5:
                di = d
                dj = -d
            else:
                di = -d
                dj = d

        if h[i] + di < -1e-12 or h[i] + di > 1.0 + 1e-12:
            continue
        if h[j] + dj < -1e-12 or h[j] + dj > 1.0 + 1e-12:
            continue

        h[i] += di
        h[j] += dj
        c2 = c5_exact(h)
        accept = c2 < c - 1e-15
        if not accept and c2 - c < T:
            if math.exp(-(c2 - c) / T) > rng.rand():
                accept = True

        if accept:
            c = c2
            if c < bestc - 1e-15:
                bestc = c
                best = h.copy()
                no = 0
            else:
                no += 1
        else:
            h[i] -= di
            h[j] -= dj
            no += 1

        if it % 20000 == 0:
            h = project_feasible(h, target)
            c = c5_exact(h)
            if c < bestc - 1e-15:
                bestc = c
                best = h.copy()

        if no > 8000:
            T = max(1e-5, T * 0.5)
            no = 0
        elif no < 2000:
            T = min(0.01, T * 1.02)

    return best, bestc


def quantize_binary(h):
    n = len(h)
    k = n // 2
    idx = np.argsort(-np.asarray(h, dtype=float))[:k]
    h2 = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    h2[idx] = 1.0
    return h2


def binary_local_search(h0, seed=7777):
    n = len(h0)
    if n < 8 or (n % 2) != 0:
        return h0, 1e9

    h0f = np.asarray(h0, dtype=float)
    k = n // 2
    idx = np.argsort(-h0f)[:k]
    h = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.int64)
    h[idx] = 1
    b = 1 - h
    c = c5_int(h, b)
    best = h.copy()
    bestc = c
    rng = np.random.RandomState(int(seed))
    T = 0.02
    no = 0
    it = 0

    while time_left() > 4.0:
        it += 1
        p = int(rng.randint(n))
        while h[p] == 0:
            p = int(rng.randint(n))
        q = int(rng.randint(n))
        while h[q] == 1:
            q = int(rng.randint(n))
        if p == q:
            continue

        h[p] -= 1
        h[q] += 1
        b[p] -= 1
        b[q] += 1

        c2 = c5_int(h, b)
        accept = c2 < c - 1e-15
        if not accept and c2 - c <= T:
            if math.exp(-(c2 - c) / T) > rng.rand():
                accept = True

        if accept:
            c = c2
            if c < bestc - 1e-15:
                bestc = c
                best = h.copy()
                no = 0
            else:
                no += 1
        else:
            h[p] += 1
            h[q] -= 1
            b[p] += 1
            b[q] -= 1
            no += 1

        if it % 5000 == 0:
            T = max(0.001, T * 0.9)
        if no > 20000:
            no = 0

    return best.astype(float), bestc


def run(seed=42, budget_s=1300, **kwargs):
    global _DEADLINE
    t0 = time.time()
    _DEADLINE = t0 + float(budget_s) - 10.0
    if _DEADLINE <= t0:
        _DEADLINE = t0 + float(budget_s) - 1.0

    best_h = None
    best_c = 1e9
    best_n = 0

    try:
        hpar = np.asarray(initial_h_values, dtype=float)
    except Exception:
        hpar = None

    # Primary construction: symmetric tapers with optional flat floor.
    if time_left() > 25.0:
        try:
            w1, c1 = optimize_shape_p1(512)
        except Exception:
            w1, c1 = 0.95, 1e9

        n_list = [256, 384, 512, 768, 1024, 2048, 4096]
        for nn in n_list:
            if time_left() < 6.0:
                break
            if nn >= 1024:
                lo = max(0.05, w1 - 0.018)
                hi = min(1.0, w1 + 0.018)
                ws = np.linspace(lo, hi, 13)
            else:
                lo = max(0.05, w1 - 0.03)
                hi = min(1.0, w1 + 0.03)
                ws = np.linspace(lo, hi, 21)

            bw = w1
            bc = 1e9
            for w in ws:
                w = float(w)
                if w <= 0.0:
                    continue
                h = shape_pw_h(nn, 1.0, w)
                h = project_feasible(h, nn / 2.0)
                c = c5_exact(h)
                if c < bc:
                    bc = c
                    bw = w

            if time_left() > 2.0:
                h = shape_pw_h(nn, 1.0, bw)
                h, c, _ = make_feasible_candidate(h)
                if h is not None and c < best_c:
                    best_h = h
                    best_c = c
                    best_n = nn

    # Use the supplied parent, if available, and simple feasible variants.
    if hpar is not None and hpar.size > 0:
        for v in transformed_variants(hpar):
            hh, cc, nn = make_feasible_candidate(v)
            if hh is not None and cc < best_c:
                best_h = hh
                best_c = cc
                best_n = nn

    # Optional broader low-dimensional search over exponent and support.
    if time_left() > 120.0:
        old_deadline = _DEADLINE
        _DEADLINE = min(old_deadline, time.time() + 160.0)
        try:
            p2, w2, c2 = optimize_shape_pw(512)
            for nn in [512, 768, 1024, 1536, 2048]:
                if time_left() < 4.0:
                    break
                h = shape_pw_h(nn, float(p2), float(w2))
                hh, cc, _ = make_feasible_candidate(h)
                if hh is not None and cc < best_c:
                    best_h = hh
                    best_c = cc
                    best_n = nn
        except Exception:
            pass
        finally:
            _DEADLINE = old_deadline

    # Local continuous polishing at moderate resolution.
    if best_h is not None and time_left() > 220.0:
        if 128 <= best_n <= 420:
            n_pol = int(best_n)
        else:
            n_pol = 320
        old_deadline = _DEADLINE
        _DEADLINE = min(old_deadline, time.time() + 300.0)
        try:
            hpol = resample_to_n(best_h, n_pol)
            hh, cc, nn = make_feasible_candidate(hpol)
            if hh is not None and cc < best_c:
                best_h = hh
                best_c = cc
                best_n = nn

            hgrad, cgrad = gradient_polish(hpol)
            if hgrad is not None:
                hh, cc, nn = make_feasible_candidate(hgrad)
                if hh is not None and cc < best_c:
                    best_h = hh
                    best_c = cc
                    best_n = nn

            if time_left() > 40.0:
                hrand, crand = random_polish(hgrad if hgrad is not None else hpol, seed + 101)
                if hrand is not None:
                    hh, cc, nn = make_feasible_candidate(hrand)
                    if hh is not None and cc < best_c:
                        best_h = hh
                        best_c = cc
                        best_n = nn
        except Exception:
            pass
        finally:
            _DEADLINE = old_deadline

    # Binary hill-climbing from parent, if useful.
    if hpar is not None and hpar.size > 0 and time_left() > 120.0:
        try:
            nbin = len(hpar)
            if nbin % 2 != 0 or nbin < 80 or nbin > 480:
                nbin = 160
                hbin_start = resample_to_n(hpar, nbin)
            else:
                hbin_start = hpar
            old_deadline = _DEADLINE
            _DEADLINE = min(old_deadline, time.time() + 200.0)
            hbin, cb = binary_local_search(hbin_start, seed + 202)
            if hbin is not None:
                hh, cc, nn = make_feasible_candidate(hbin)
                if hh is not None and cc < best_c:
                    best_h = hh
                    best_c = cc
                    best_n = nn
        except Exception:
            pass
        finally:
            _DEADLINE = old_deadline

    # Fallback.
    if best_h is None:
        h = shape_pw_h(512, 1.0, 0.95)
        best_h, best_c, best_n = make_feasible_candidate(h)
        if best_h is None:
            best_h = np.full(128, 0.5, dtype=float)
            best_h = project_feasible(best_h, 64.0)
            best_c = c5_exact(best_h)
            best_n = 128

    best_h = project_feasible(best_h, best_n / 2.0)
    best_c = c5_exact(best_h)

    try:
        eval_c = evaluate_erdos_solution(best_h)
        if eval_c is not None and float(eval_c) < best_c:
            best_c = float(eval_c)
    except Exception:
        pass

    return (best_h, best_c, best_n)
```
